- **nda-position-check**: Prepare, for the company's named lawyer, a clause-by-clause comparison of one proposed NDA with the positions that lawyer has approved in writing for the company's side of the exchange. What it produces is a draft for counsel. It never says the NDA can be signed: counsel decides and signs, on every NDA, including one that matches every position.
